sort(a, l, r):
    if r - l <= 1:
        return
    x = a[random(l, r - 1)]
    m = Partitions(a, l, r, x)
    sort(a, l, m)
    sort(a, m, r) ^LkSJhDdN

Partitions ^rugHIMIr

int partitions(a, k, r, x)
    m = l
    for i = 0; i < r; i++
        if a[i] < x
            swap(a[m++], a[i])
    return m     ^ZthCACMZ

find(a, k, l, r):
    x = a[random(l, r-1)]
    if m == k:
        return a[m]
    m = partitions(a, l, k, x)
    if k < m:
        return find(a, k, l, m)
    else:
        return find(a, k, r, m) ^cSky8r0J
